### Pireel OSS editor components · AGPL-3.0

Building a self-hosted or bring-your-own-agent video editing surface.
Open editor/engine components assemble AI editing briefs, transcript context and chat/provider interfaces for a caller-supplied model or agent. The shipped OSS shell leaves hosted AI capabilities unavailable by default.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Complete AGPL3 code grant reviewed. The actual shell provides local uploads while inheriting unavailable AI providers; chat, ASR, generation and persistence need host implementations. The hosted product backend, production recipes and billing are not made open by this release. This is a developer component, not an out-of-box OSS replacement for every hosted AI feature.

### pixel-engine · MIT

Agent-authored small pixel scenes and frame-by-frame animations.
A coding LLM constructs palette/layer scene JSON and iterates using pixel/ASCII inspection; the zero-dependency engine deterministically rasterizes it.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. The owner explicitly calls this an experimental research prototype. Node 14 badge, Node 24 development and 18/20/22 CI statements differ in scope.

### Game Assets Maker · MIT

Turning short videos or generated character clips into inspected transparent animation frames.
Optional Seedream/Seedance generation and local U2Net background removal feed transparent sprite-frame cleanup, with agent and Godot handoff tooling.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Current installers target Windows x64 and Apple Silicon macOS; no Intel Mac/Linux package is supplied. U2Net downloads about 176 MB once; hosted generation is separately billed. Source-video rights, model terms, unsigned Mac packaging and alignment-induced motion changes need review. A user reports that the same video loses much of its subject in desktop background removal while the Codex gam-workflow result works better. GUI/skill parity and exact configuration remain unresolved.

### anima · MIT

Experimental character-head reconstruction and pixel-art yaw/expression studies.
The offline forge combines learned SAM segmentation and face landmarks with painted-view projection and Blender geometry, then quantizes rendered heads to a five-grey sprite palette.

### StemStudio · MIT

### EasyComfee · MIT

Trying model-specific creative video transformations with a simpler interface.
A simplified ComfyUI studio connects LTX and other neural workflows for shot generation, edits, relighting, lip sync and sound.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Code licensing does not clear its mixed model catalog: multiple weights use NC, community or other conditions, and Comfy Cloud/partner nodes are services. Owner16 GB examples are not universal minima; beta blockout-to-video and relight claims need workflow-specific testing. Full install and model provenance review remain queued. A user reports Comfy Desktop discovery failure and a cloud-only account lacking a local server; the issue is closed, but closure alone does not reproduce the fix. A2 GB GPU is not a sufficient basis for all advertised local models.

Organizing auditable story development and scene-planning alternatives.
Agents produce story text, scripts and textual storyboards through versioned workflows with human review; image-service adapters are separately scoped.

### Lottie Animator · MIT

Making editable icon animations, loaders and small vector motion assets.
An agent skill/MCP server authors and revises Lottie recipes, converts SVG, lints known rendering defects and inspects actual frame/contact-sheet output.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. MIT code; Python 3.8+ tools are dependency-free, while rendering needs Node/Chrome and npm dependencies. New plugin syntax requires a current compatible Claude client; copied text without linked scripts degrades to guidance. Lint and owner tests do not verify all native Lottie players or visual taste.

### Motion Educator · MIT

Drafting fact-sourced educational explainers with editable scenes.
An AI-agent skill plans a storyboard, authors time-addressable 3D/HTML film scenes, and combines optional neural narration with captions and music.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. MIT engine code does not license GSAP, Mixkit downloads, ElevenLabs voices or all reference media. The later collected README adds BodyParts3D anatomy and tissue/molecular visualization modules. Those optional assets need separate provenance, permission and scientific-accuracy review; the code grant and cinematic presentation do not establish anatomical accuracy.

### ACE-Step Studio · MIT

Exploring local song sketches and alternate takes on supported NVIDIA hardware.
A native acestep.cpp frontend runs ACE-Step 1.5 prompt/lyric song synthesis, with optional covers, repaints, stems and MIDI tools.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. MIT studio/base model claims do not cover every optional fine-tune: Regrind/decoders are NC-SA, MuScriptor weights NC, catalog LoRAs vary, and an audio visualizer is AGPL. Current Windows x64 native runtime differs from old Python/Node 1.x setup;4 GB owner claims require exact model/configuration review. A current 4.0.1 Windows portable user reports the cuBLAS download returning 403 even on an RTX5090/64 GB machine. Other sampled reports include degraded cover output, a silence-producing Regrind variant and a startup failure. More GPU memory does not resolve every setup/model path.

### QVAC · Apache-2.0

Building a model-backed creative-media tool rather than using a hosted-only API.
An SDK/local model provider exposes neural image/video and speech inference so creators can build local-first media applications.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Complete Apache 2 operative grant reviewed. Broad Linux/macOS/Windows/Android/iOS claims are backend/model-specific; exact image/video example, accelerator support and model licenses need a detailed guide. SDK breadth is not proof that every workload runs on every device. The collected README now explicitly shares the @qvac/fabric runtime across native speech and LLM addons; speech engines compile against its headers and GPU backends load through Fabric. Runtime/addon/platform compatibility remains a separate unverified requirement. A first-hand CLI0.18/SDK0.23 report says startup re-downloads a cached model and a timeout leaves a truncated file. Current cache recovery and model/backend behavior need confirmation.

### ComfyUI fal API · Apache-2.0

Composing paid hosted inference into an inspectable ComfyUI workflow.
Open-source ComfyUI nodes submit neural image/video/audio/3D generation jobs to fal services and retain job/output provenance.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Complete Apache 2 code grant reviewed. fal account, model endpoints, input uploads and usage fees are separate; cache/spend guards do not guarantee no provider charges after every failure. Automatically generated endpoint counts are a snapshot, not a quality claim; full compatibility review remains queued. A user reports registry refresh blocked by removed schema choices; current README2.5.1 describes a preservation/update fix, but a closed issue and release description do not independently reproduce every endpoint.

### DUGCO Tools · MIT

Integrating AI-generated footage into a VFX finishing workflow while keeping original plate pixels where possible.
ComfyUI nodes/workflows carry neural video outputs, relit passes, latents and AI-model provenance into finishing, plate transfer and EXR delivery.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. MIT nodes include conventional CPU image/flow/lighting operations; they are not themselves a neural relight model. The older standalone Plate Relight moved here and is not separately admitted. ComfyUI/models, optional nodes, RAM-heavy float batches and exact workflow grants need per-tool review. A first-hand Windows ComfyUI 0.45/OpenEXR 3.4.4 report finds zero chromaticities in exported EXRs; the upstream legacy API truncation is also reported for newer OpenEXR. Channels/bit depth/compression matching does not clear color metadata. The issue is open.
